## პროექტის მიზანი
Python/FastAPI AI Sales Agent რომელიც მუშაობს Instagram DM, Facebook Messenger
და WhatsApp-ზე. სიტყვის აკადემიის კონცეპტუალური დამკვეთისთვის,
მაგრამ აიგო template-ად — სხვა კომპანიაზე გადატანა მხოლოდ .env + /data
+ `app/agent/knowledge/*.yaml` შეცვლით უნდა მოხდეს.

## არქიტექტურა
- **Backend:** FastAPI + Uvicorn (Python 3.10.11)
- **LLM:** OpenAI GPT-4.1-mini
  - **P3-C engine** (`USE_PARENT_LLM_ENGINE=true`) — function calling, LLM აზროვნებს და ბექენდ ხელსაწყოებს იძახებს. PARENT flow-ისთვის. 8 tools (camp info, slots, book, manage cancel/reschedule, manager, save lead, adult switch, **check_consultation_slot — PATCH 6**).
  - Legacy: composer / analyzer / start-intent (all retained for fallback).
- **Messaging:** Meta Graph API v19.0 (one stale v18.0 in `notification_service.py`)
- **CRM:** Google Sheets (gspread) — timestamps in Asia/Tbilisi (`+04:00`)
- **Booking:** Google Calendar API (Asia/Tbilisi). `book_slot` stashes `event_id` on lead; `cancel_calendar_event` for safe cancel/reschedule.
- **Comment flow:** Meta webhook → post-caption hashtag → `admin_config_service.find_section_from_post_hashtags` (primary) / legacy `settings.PARENT/ADULT_HASHTAGS` (fallback) → `INTERESTED` / `NOT_INTERESTED` intent → optional public reply (gated by `ENABLE_PUBLIC_COMMENT_REPLY`) → **private reply** (`recipient={"comment_id": …}` to `/{PAGE_ID}/messages`) → rich first-contact DM (admin_config template first, canonical camp_2026.yaml second, fallback constants last) → user replies in Messenger → normal parent / adult flow continues. New `processed_comment:{id}` Redis guard short-circuits Meta webhook re-deliveries.
- **Admin Panel:** `/admin` (Jinja2 + HTTP Basic Auth via `ADMIN_USERNAME` / `ADMIN_PASSWORD`, gated by `ADMIN_PANEL_ENABLED`, default OFF). 8 routes covering dashboard / programs / templates / settings. Operator edits to price, streams, location, registration URL flow through `admin_config_service.get_camp_facts()` to the LLM's `get_camp_info` tool and to the comment rich DM — no Python code edits required for new sections.
- **Email notification:** Gmail SMTP (App Password required). `notification_service._send_email` with explicit gates (`ENABLE_EMAIL_NOTIFICATIONS`, missing-MANAGER_EMAIL, missing-SMTP-creds), specific `SMTPAuthenticationError` → "Gmail App Password required" hint. Body builder is programmatic: `_georgian_genitive()` inflects company name; `deeper_concern` line shown only when meaningful and not duplicate; LLM narrative replaced with a short fixed Georgian summary; new contact-info block.
- **Persistence:** Redis-backed (P3-B). `redis_state_service` lazy-connects, never logs the password. `conversation_service` write-through. `manager_notified` flag mirrored. `processed_comment:{id}` guard. Safe-fallback to in-memory only when `REDIS_URL` empty OR `REDIS_ENABLED=false` OR Redis unreachable.
- **Scheduler:** APScheduler (every hour — followup + comment followup). Sender NOT yet wired to PATCH 3 markers.
- **Follow-up state:** Conversation has 5 readiness fields (`last_bot_message_at`, `followup_stage`, `followup_blocked_reason`, `last_meaningful_interest`, `stopped_after`). Cadence is data-only — scheduler not yet wired to them.
- **Debounce:** 5–15s message buffer (per-sender, in-memory)
- **Deploy:** Railway (planned). Single-worker recommended for v1 (Redis is in place, but per-process in-memory dicts would race across workers).

## Source-of-truth canonical sources (2026-06-22, after Tasks 1→5A-3)
Live user-facing camp facts now flow through ONE canonical source each — `camp_2026.yaml` is no longer a LIVE PRIMARY source (fallback / legacy / boot-default / test only):
- **stream dates** → `get_camp_info` / `get_visible_camp_streams` date-filter (NOT prompt literals — Task 1).
- **Sunday-School status** → `sections.yaml` `sunday_school` via `admin_config_service.get_sunday_school_status()` (Task 2); Admin-Panel `save_program` PRESERVES those + unknown keys (Task 3).
- **manager phone** → `admin_config_service.get_manager_phone()`; `get_camp_facts()['phone']` defers to it (Task 4).
- **camp age band** → `admin_config_service.get_camp_age_bounds()` across ALL 6 live readers (prompt context, eligibility, under-age, booking, adult-switch — Tasks 5A-1/5A-2).
- **post-booking facts** → `get_camp_facts()` (Task 5A-3); **comment rich DM** is admin-first via `build_section_dm` (camp_2026 fallback only).
- `parent_flow` has ZERO direct `camp_2026.yaml` reads. Behavioural gaps now addressed (2026-06-23): PARENT prompt-injection/off-topic guard (`_maybe_handle_offtopic_injection`), state recall, handoff/contact intent priority + semantic name validation. Still open: router normalization / Latin-Georgian transliteration (simple cases done), negated-event recovery, PARENT→ADULT deterministic switch. **Reasoning Layer (UPDATED 2026-06-23): Phase 1 IMPLEMENTED, gated, default OFF.** Module `app/reasoning/reasoning_layer.py` (+ `__init__.py`); flag **`USE_REASONING_LAYER`** (code default False, pinned OFF in conftest). DETERMINISTIC, metadata-only intent analyzer (`analyze_parent_turn`) — NOT a free-form answer generator, NO LLM call, no side effects, fail-closed; never overrides deterministic handlers; Phase-1 wiring = decline+topic-switch deferral only. `docs/REASONING_LAYER_BRIEF.md` + `docs/reference/reasoning_layer.py` describe the FUTURE LLM-based `analyze→ground→answer→reflect` vision (brief's `USE_REASONING_PASS` is superseded by `USE_REASONING_LAYER`); the reference scaffold is reference-only and NOT imported into production.

## ფაილების სტრუქტურა

### Core
- `app/main.py` — FastAPI entry + APScheduler init + flag print at boot + `redis_state_service.log_startup_status()` + mounts `admin_router`
- `app/config.py` — Settings env reader + LLM/comment/email/Redis/admin flags. **`_env` is Railway-safe (2026-06-18): reads `os.environ` FIRST, then `.env` fallback** (Railway injects dashboard vars into `os.environ`; there is no `.env` file in the container). `load_dotenv` still populates `os.environ` from `.env` locally, so dev behaviour is unchanged. `REDIS_URL` / `LIVE_BROADCAST_ENABLED` / all vars read through this. Never logs values.
- `app/routes/webhook.py` ⭐ **P3-B** — Meta webhook (GET verify + POST messages/comments). PATCH 1 debug logs + Comment Flow PATCH 1/2 decoupled public reply + comment_id-aware DM + **`processed_comment:{id}` Redis duplicate guard**.
- `app/routes/admin.py` ⭐ **Admin Panel MVP** — 8 routes (dashboard / programs / new / edit / delete / templates / settings) with HTTP Basic Auth. Starlette 0.49-compatible `TemplateResponse(request, name, context)` form. Form parser derives `price_gel` from `price_text`, parses streams / included_items / discounts textareas.

### Services
- `app/services/openai_service.py` — segment detect + start intent + composer + summary + **`chat_with_tools` (P3-C SAFE)** + **Georgian-only `generate_summary` with English fallback (PATCH 2)**
- `app/services/conversation_service.py` ⭐ **P3-B** — in-memory store + routing + segment classifier + **follow-up marker capture (PATCH 3)** + **`reset_conversation_for_sender` (PATCH 7)** + **Redis write-through on every `process_message`, restore on miss** + **registration-link clarification (`_is_registration_link_request`): a fresh target-less registration request (UNCLEAR) asks „ბანაკის თუ კონკრეტული ღონისძიების?" instead of guessing; `„ფორმა"` token-aware (`_REGISTRATION_FORM_TOKEN_RE`) so „ინფორმაცია" never triggers it**
- `app/services/comment_service.py` ⭐ **Comment Flow PATCH 1/2/3 + Admin Panel MVP** — hashtag normaliser, INTERESTED classifier, private-reply routing, rich first-contact DM builders (`_build_parent_rich_dm` reads admin_config first, falls back to `camp_2026.yaml`), `_build_adult_rich_dm` from `data/events.txt`, `_locative_location`, safe YAML fallbacks, `resolve_section_from_post` + admin-aware `send_dm_from_comment`, `determine_segment_from_post` consults admin sections first
- `app/services/messenger_service.py` — Meta Graph send + profile fetch + **`send_private_reply(comment_id, text)` (Comment Flow PATCH 2)** — posts to `/{PAGE_ID}/messages` with `recipient={"comment_id": …}` for comment-origin DMs. **WhatsApp send resolves token/phone-id via `settings.get_whatsapp_access_token()` / `get_whatsapp_phone_number_id()` (alias-aware). `get_user_profile` masks `access_token` in error logs (`_mask_access_token`); profile-fetch failure is non-blocking (returns `{}`).**
- `app/services/google_credentials.py` ⭐ **Railway-safe Google creds (2026-06-13)** — single `load_google_credentials(scopes, *, file_value="")` resolver used by BOTH Sheets and Calendar. `GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS_JSON` (`os.environ`) wins → `from_service_account_info` (with `private_key` escaped-`\n` repair); else per-service inline-JSON/path `file_value`; else `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` path → `from_service_account_file`; else clear secret-free error. NEVER logs credential contents.
- `app/services/sheets_service.py` — gspread CRM (⚠️ `get_cold_leads` naive-datetime bug — line ~164). Auth via `_sheets_client()` → `gspread.authorize(load_google_credentials(SHEETS_SCOPES, ...))` (single chokepoint; the 3 worksheet helpers share it). **Leads append is A-anchored (P0 Sheets-alignment fix, 2026-06-18): `save_lead` writes `worksheet.update(range_name="A{n}:Q{n}", ...)` via `_append_lead_row_aligned` — NOT gspread's unbounded `append_row`, which let `values.append` table-detection shift rows under the wrong headers. `_lead_to_row` order = `HEADERS` (17 cols A–Q); schema unchanged.**
- `app/services/calendar_service.py` — booking slots + create event + **`cancel_calendar_event` (PATCH 1)** + **`get_free_slots(start_date=..., days=...)` (PATCH 4)** + **`is_within_business_hours` + `check_slot_calendar_only` + today-only `SLOT_BUFFER` (PATCH 6)** + **`is_closed_booking_day` / `CLOSED_WEEKDAYS = {6}` (Saturday Scheduling Policy, 2026-06-16 — single source of truth: bookings allowed Mon–Sat, only Sunday closed; `is_within_business_hours` still returns reason `"weekend"` for Sunday)**. Auth via `_calendar_service()` → `build("calendar","v3", credentials=load_google_credentials([CALENDAR_SCOPE], ...))`.
- `app/services/notification_service.py` ⭐ **Booking Notification QA + Email Wording Patch** — Gmail SMTP with explicit config gates + Gmail App Password hint; programmatic email body builder (`_georgian_genitive`, conditional `deeper_concern`, fixed Georgian summary, contact-info block, Georgian booking-datetime). v18 URL still hardcoded for WhatsApp (issue #7).
- `app/services/redis_state_service.py` ⭐ **P3-B** — lazy-connect Redis client + `get_json / set_json / delete / exists / ping / log_startup_status`. Password never logged. Safe-fallback to no-op when disabled/unavailable.
- `app/services/kill_switch.py` ⭐ **Kill Switch** — canonical `AGENT_ENABLED` gate. Single Georgian `AGENT_DISABLED_MESSAGE` constant. `is_agent_enabled()` resolved per-call from `settings.AGENT_ENABLED` (default-on if missing). Used by `conversation_service.process_message`, `webhook.handle_comment`, `followup_service.check_and_send_followups`. Safe `[kill_switch]` log lines mask sender ids.
- `app/services/sentry_service.py` ⭐ **Error Monitoring** — optional Sentry wrapper. `init_sentry()`, `capture_exception(exc, context)`, `capture_message()`, `set_tag()`, `mask_sender()`. SDK availability resolved at module import (`_SDK_AVAILABLE`); init success tracked in `_INITIALIZED`. Every public function is a safe no-op when DSN empty OR sentry-sdk missing OR init failed. `send_default_pii=False`, `attach_stacktrace=True`. FastApi + Asyncio integrations attempted best-effort. Three capture points only: `conversation_service.process_message` (wrapper around `_process_message_impl` — re-raises after capture so webhook still sees the failure), `parent_tool_executor.execute` (existing except block), `followup_service.check_and_send_followups` (per-conversation except block). Stage in the Sentry context is STRING (`first_24h` / `second_3d` / `third_7d`), matching `followup_strategy.yaml`.
- `app/services/admin_config_service.py` ⭐ **Admin Panel MVP + Config Unification + Field Completion + Camp Stream Date Filter** — loader, hashtag router, template renderer, validator, write API with `.bak` rotation. `get_camp_facts()` admin-first merge over `camp_2026.yaml` (left RAW — the data source). `parse_price_gel()` for price sync. `_SafeDict` for crash-free placeholder rendering. **Camp Stream Date Filter (2026-06-20): `is_camp_stream_visible()` / `get_visible_camp_streams()` / `_parse_camp_stream_start_date()` hide a camp stream once its Asia/Tbilisi start date arrives (active AND today<start → visible; today≥start OR inactive → hidden; range „DD-DD month" → first day; empty=visible, unparseable=hidden+warn). Display/eligibility filter ONLY — never deletes/mutates config or KB. Reuses `_find_month` + `_now_tbilisi`. Applied at `_get_camp_info` (tool), composer/analyzer/turn-router/post-booking facts, comment rich-DM, and `render_section_dm` (camp-type sections only).**
- `app/services/followup_service.py` ⭐ **Follow-up Scheduler Patch** — Conversation-marker-driven 24h / 72h / 168h cadence. Stages `"" → "first_24h" → "second_3d" → "third_7d"` match `followup_strategy.yaml` keys. Skips on blocked reasons (booked / declined / manager_handoff_completed / asked_no_more_messages / followup_exhausted / registered) + non-PARENT segment + missing sender / `last_bot_message_at` / unsupported platform. Sends via `messenger_service.send_message` preserving the Conversation's `platform`. Admin template (`followup_24h` / `followup_3d` / `followup_7d`) wins; safe Georgian fallback constants used on miss / render error. Kill-switch gated. Failed sends still advance the stage to avoid retry loops.
- `app/services/message_buffer.py` — 5–15s debounce

### Flows
- `app/flows/parent_flow.py` — **P3-C engine gate** + **PATCH 5/6/7/8 hooks** (static welcome bypass, decline handler, time-change handler, pending-booking commit, ineligible-age CTA scrubber). Engine runs first when flag is true; legacy state machine fallback. DONE handler. Fake-booking guard (tool-success gated since PATCH 5). **Camp registration LIVE-PATH (2026-06-19/20): `_maybe_handle_camp_registration_link` returns the Admin `registration_url` DETERMINISTICALLY (pre-engine, no age question, no menu) for a clear camp registration/link/form request; `„ფორმა"` is token-aware (`_CAMP_FORM_TOKEN_RE`, word boundary) so an INFORMATION request („ინფორმაცია") never over-fires the link.** **Live-Demo Polish (2026-06-21/22), pre-engine interceptors (after underage-handoff, before contact-collection): `_maybe_handle_explicit_manager_request` discloses `get_manager_phone()` (558 67 47 33) on `მენეჯერ`+`ნომერ/ტელეფონ/კონტაქტ`+no-own-phone, via context-aware `_render_manager_number_answer(lead)` (no re-ask when phone known); `_maybe_handle_contact_correction` overwrites `lead.phone` / `lead.name` on an explicit correction (`შევცდი/ეს არა/სხვა ნომერ` / `კი არა` + last valid value), in-memory only, never touches Calendar/Sheets; `_maybe_handle_decline_engine` now defers to the engine (NOT decline) when a decline phrase co-occurs with `_DECLINE_OVERRIDE_INTEREST = (მაგრამ, თუმცა, მაინც, ძვირ, მიჭირს)` → price objections keep selling; `_maybe_request_full_contact_on_intent` varies wording on a repeated contact-ask (`_CONTACT_REQUEST_*_RETRY`).**
- `app/flows/parent_turn_router.py` — silent intent router + pending booking + booking attempt (legacy path only)
- `app/flows/adult_flow.py` — ADULT state machine + **PATCH 7 global intent guard** (identity / human-vs-robot / greeting / thanks / decline / manager). Still no LLM engine; never live-tested end-to-end.

### Agent layer
- `app/agent/intent/parent_intent_detector.py` — closed-set Georgian stem detector (P0)
- `app/agent/llm/parent_reply_composer.py` — discovery composer + post-booking composer (Phase 3.8 + P2)
- `app/agent/llm/parent_turn_analyzer.py` — LLM JSON-classifier (Phase 3.9)
- `app/agent/llm/parent_llm_engine.py` ⭐ **P3-C + Consultation Flow Memory** — LLM tool-calling loop + `sanitise_response_wording` (60+ rewrites across PATCH 1–8) + situation-aware sales context (`_build_sales_context`) + pending_booking context exposure (PATCH 5). **Consultation Flow Memory / Repeated Age Fix (2026-06-20): `maybe_capture_child_age_fallback` now strips phones (`_strip_phone_numbers`) BEFORE age parsing so „14 წლის არის 595999733" captures the age (the old `_PHONE_HINT_TOKENS` „595…" guard bailed and the agent re-asked the age forever); new `maybe_capture_phone_fallback` (one valid 9-digit phone, never overwrites/ never touches child_age); `_capture_turn_facts` runs PRE-turn at the top of `run_parent_llm_turn` (before `_build_context_message`/`_build_sales_context`) so the model sees both facts the same turn; `_suppress_redundant_age_question` anti-repeat guard replaces a „რამდენი წლის"/„რა ასაკის" question with the next missing detail (phone, else day/time) when child_age is already known. Canonical fields `lead.child_age` / `lead.phone`. Pre-booking age correction („არა, 15 წლისაა") preserved. No prompt change — the prompt already forbade re-asking known facts.** **Live-Demo Polish (2026-06-21/22): `_strip_mid_conversation_greeting` removes a sentence-initial „გამარჯობა/სალამი/მოგესალმებით" from the engine's reply once the conversation already has an assistant turn (the static welcome owns the FIRST greeting) — applied right after `_suppress_redundant_age_question` in `run_parent_llm_turn`.**
- `app/agent/llm/adult_llm_engine.py` ⭐ **ADULT Engine** — ADULT LLM tool-calling loop + `sanitise_adult_response` (genitive fix + banned greeting / opener / retail / pressure-word strips) + deterministic adult-to-parent switch on camp keywords + child-age-in-range. Fail/empty → legacy `adult_flow.handle` state machine.
- `app/agent/llm/prompt_loader.py` — markdown prompt loader
- `app/agent/tools/parent_tools.py` ⭐ **P3-C** — 8 tool schemas (`get_camp_info`, `get_available_slots`, `book_consultation`, `manage_consultation_booking`, `request_manager_callback`, `save_lead_info`, `switch_to_adult_flow`, **`check_consultation_slot` — PATCH 6**)
- `app/agent/tools/parent_tool_executor.py` ⭐ **P3-C** — `ParentToolExecutor` backend validator/executor for all 8 tools + `book_consultation_success_for_conversation` per-turn flag (PATCH 5) + phone-mask logs
- `app/agent/tools/adult_tools.py` ⭐ **ADULT Engine** — 6 tool schemas (`get_adult_events`, `get_adult_event_details`, `save_adult_lead_info`, `request_adult_manager_callback`, `provide_adult_reservation_link`, `switch_to_parent_flow`)
- `app/agent/tools/adult_tool_executor.py` ⭐ **ADULT Engine** — `AdultToolExecutor` backend validator/executor for all 6 tools + `adult_manager_notified_for_conversation` guard (idempotent per conversation, mirrored to Redis) + manager-phone disclosure via `admin_config_service.get_manager_phone()` chain. ABSOLUTELY does NOT touch Google Calendar.
- `app/agent/services/knowledge_loader.py` — YAML knowledge loader (cached)
- `app/agent/services/template_loader.py` — YAML template loader (cached)
- `app/agent/services/timestamps.py` — Asia/Tbilisi datetime formatter

- `app/models/lead.py` — Lead dataclass + `from_dict` + `booked_datetime_iso` + **`calendar_event_id` (PATCH 1)**

### Policies (`app/agent/policies/*.md`)
- `parent_sales_policy.md` ⭐ **PATCH 3** — 12-rule operational sales policy (role, conversation principle, age first, motivation discovery, price rule, value before CTA, decline rule, adult interest, tone, audience-aware adapters)
- `adult_sales_policy.md` ⭐ **ADULT Engine** — 12-section operational sales policy (role, conversation principle, age-question phrasing — შვილისთვის vs ჩემთვის, polite question for age outside camp range, facts rule, reservation rule, manager-handoff rule, switch back to parent, decline rule, tone, grammar, future verticals pattern)

### Knowledge YAML (`app/agent/knowledge/`)
- `camp_2026.yaml` — authoritative camp facts (age_min: 9, age_max: 17, price 2150, location "ამბასადორი კაჭრეთი", streams, registration_url). Loaded by `_build_parent_rich_dm` for the comment DM.
- `company.yaml` — company name + phone
- `business_hours.yaml` — TZ (Asia/Tbilisi), work_hours 10:00–21:00, business_hours 10:00–21:00, slot 60 min (last start 20:00), buffer 120 min (PATCH 6: today only). Booking days = Mon–Sat (Sunday closed) — the weekday policy lives in code (`calendar_service.is_closed_booking_day`), not this YAML.
- `adult_defaults.yaml` — adult event placeholders
- `i18n/ka_months.yaml` — Georgian month names + stems
- `audience_segments.yaml` ⭐ **PATCH 3** — 4 segments + 3 micro
- `followup_strategy.yaml` ⭐ **PATCH 3** — 3 cadence stages + scenario followups

### Templates (`app/agent/templates/`)
- `parent/*.yaml`, `adult/*.yaml`, `common/*.yaml`, `comments/replies.yaml` (PATCH 3 — `reply_dm_sent` text is now uniform, no `{name}` placeholder), `notifications/manager.yaml`, `calendar/event.yaml`

### Backwards-compat
- `data/prompts.py` — alias layer (`PARENT_WELCOME = _t("parent","welcome")`)
- `data/knowledge_base.txt`, `data/events.txt` — text templates. `data/events.txt` is parsed by `comment_service._parse_events_blocks` for the ADULT rich DM.

### Admin config (operator-editable)
- `data/admin_config/sections.yaml` ⭐ — section registry: summer_camp, sunday_school, adult_events. Edited from `/admin/programs/{id}`.
- `data/admin_config/templates.yaml` ⭐ — DM / public-reply / follow-up wording templates. Edited from `/admin/templates`.
- `data/admin_config/business_hours.yaml` ⭐ — operator-visible mirror of booking hours (canonical source remains `app/agent/knowledge/business_hours.yaml`).
- `data/admin_config/manager_contacts.yaml` ⭐ — non-secret placeholders only (real SMTP / Meta / OpenAI credentials stay in `.env`).

### Admin Panel templates
- `templates/admin/base.html`, `dashboard.html`, `programs.html`, `program_form.html`, `templates.html`, `settings.html` ⭐ — Jinja2 + inline CSS, no JS framework, no build step.

## ენა და ტექსტი
- კოდის კომენტარები: ქართულად ან ინგლისურად — OK
- მომხმარებლისთვის ნაჩვენები ტექსტი: **მხოლოდ** `app/agent/templates/**/*.yaml`-ში (legacy alias: `data/prompts.py`)
- ფაქტები (ფასი, ლოკაცია, ასაკი): **მხოლოდ** `app/agent/knowledge/*.yaml`-ში
- სალამური წესები: **მხოლოდ** `app/agent/policies/parent_sales_policy.md`-ში
- აუდიტორიის სეგმენტები: **მხოლოდ** `app/agent/knowledge/audience_segments.yaml`-ში
- არასოდეს დაამატო hardcoded ქართული string-ი Python ფაილში (გამონაკლისები: `comment_service.PARENT_FIRST_CONTACT_DM` და `ADULT_NO_EVENTS_DM` — გამიზნულად SAFE FALLBACK არიან rich-DM builder-ის გარეშე)
- ცვლადები prompt template-ებში: `{var_name}` ფორმატით (არა `{var-name}`)

## Feature flags

| Flag | Default (code) | Default (.env) | Effect when true |
|---|---|---|---|
| `USE_LLM_TURN_ANALYZER` | `False` | **`true`** | LLM analyzer fallback when deterministic detector returns None |
| `USE_LLM_COMPOSER` | `False` | `false` | LLM rewrites the 4 discovery turns + powers `compose_post_booking_response` |
| `USE_PARENT_LLM_ENGINE` ⭐ **P3-C** | `False` | **`true`** (live) | LLM tool-calling engine handles PARENT flow first. Engine fail/empty → legacy fallback. Tests pin OFF via `tests/conftest.py`. |
| `USE_REASONING_LAYER` ⭐ **Reasoning Layer Phase 1 (2026-06-23)** | `False` | (unset → `false`) | When true, the gated DETERMINISTIC intent analyzer (`app/reasoning/reasoning_layer.analyze_parent_turn`) classifies ambiguous PARENT turns into structured metadata (segment/topic/intent/question/affirmation/decline/topic-switch + requested_action + confidence). Metadata-only — NEVER user-facing text, NO LLM call, no side effects, fail-closed; never overrides a high-confidence deterministic handler. Phase-1 live use = decline+topic-switch deferral only. Default OFF + pinned OFF in `tests/conftest.py`; scenario_runner runs with it OFF (byte-identical). |
| `USE_ADULT_LLM_ENGINE` ⭐ **ADULT Engine** | **`True`** | **`true`** (live) | ADULT LLM tool-calling engine (`app/agent/llm/adult_llm_engine.py`) handles ADULT flow first. 6 tools (events list / event details / save lead / manager handoff / reservation link / parent switch). Engine fail/empty → legacy `adult_flow` state machine. NO Calendar booking in this flow. Per-event `min_age` lives in `data/admin_config/sections.yaml`. Manager phone is sourced via `admin_config_service.get_manager_phone()` — never hard-coded. Tests pin OFF via `tests/conftest.py` so the legacy ADULT state-machine tests keep running unmodified. |
| `ENABLE_PUBLIC_COMMENT_REPLY` ⭐ **Comment Follow-up Logic Fix (2026-05-31)** | **`True`** | **`true`** | When true, comment handler attempts a public reply alongside the private DM. Code default flipped to True so public replies auto-activate once Meta App Review grants `pages_manage_engagement` and the Page Access Token is refreshed — no further code change needed. Until that happens Meta returns HTTP 400 for the public-reply POST; the handler logs safely and the private DM still goes out. `.env` set to `false` to force-disable per deploy. |
| `ENABLE_EMAIL_NOTIFICATIONS` ⭐ **Booking Notification QA** | `True` | **`true`** (live) | Gmail SMTP email after booking. Requires `SMTP_HOST/PORT/USERNAME/PASSWORD/FROM_EMAIL` + `MANAGER_EMAIL`. `SMTP_PASSWORD` MUST be a Gmail **App Password**. |
| `ADMIN_PANEL_ENABLED` ⭐ **Admin Panel MVP** | `False` | `true` (local) | Mounts `/admin` Jinja2 routes with HTTP Basic Auth. Returns 404 when off, 503 when on+no-password. |
| `REDIS_ENABLED` ⭐ **P3-B** | `True` | **`true`** (live) | Redis persistence of Conversation, manager-notified guard, processed-comment guard. When OFF or `REDIS_URL` empty the app silently runs in-memory only. Tests pin OFF via conftest autouse fixture. |
| `AGENT_ENABLED` ⭐ **Kill Switch** | `True` | **`true`** (live) | Emergency disable. When `false` the DM/comment/follow-up entry points return `kill_switch.AGENT_DISABLED_MESSAGE` and skip every OpenAI / Calendar / Sheets / email / Meta-send call. `/webhook` GET verify, `/health`, and `/admin` are NOT gated by this flag. Missing `.env` line → defaults to `true`. |
| `LIVE_BROADCAST_ENABLED` ⭐ **Broadcast Safety (2026-06-11)** | `False` | (unset → `false`) | Master switch for OUTBOUND adult-event broadcasts. When `false` (DEFAULT) `adult_event_broadcast_service.broadcast_event` runs DRY-RUN: resolves the event + loads candidates but NEVER calls `messenger_service.send_message` and NEVER marks subscribers notified (`result["dry_run"]=True`). Prevents tests / scripts / dev machines (which may hold real Meta + Sheets creds) from DMing real subscribers. **Production must set `true` for real fan-out.** Incident 2026-06-11: an unmocked broadcast test sent a real DM — this flag is the hard safety net. |
| `SENTRY_DSN` ⭐ **Sentry** | `""` | empty (disabled) | When non-empty AND `sentry-sdk` is installed, exceptions from `conversation_service.process_message`, `parent_tool_executor.execute`, and `followup_service.check_and_send_followups` are forwarded to Sentry with privacy-safe context (area, platform, tool, follow-up stage as STRING, masked sender id). Missing `.env` line OR missing `sentry-sdk` → Sentry disabled, app boots normally. |
| `VERIFY_WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE` ⭐ **Webhook Signature** | `True` | **`true`** (live) | When True AND `META_APP_SECRET` (alias `MESSENGER_APP_SECRET`) is non-empty, every `POST /webhook` body is verified against the `X-Hub-Signature-256` HMAC-SHA256 header (constant-time compare). Verification failure → `403 Forbidden`, no background task scheduled. Fail-open: when flag is True but the app secret is empty, one `[webhook] signature check skipped` warning is logged and the request proceeds — protects legacy / local-dev installs and the existing test suite that doesn't sign requests. Setting the flag False unconditionally skips verification. |
| `SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT` ⭐ **Sentry** | `"production"` | `"production"` | Forwarded to `sentry_sdk.init`. Operator can set `qa` / `staging` per deploy. |
| `SENTRY_TRACES_SAMPLE_RATE` ⭐ **Sentry** | `0.0` | `0.0` | Float in `[0.0, 1.0]`. Out-of-range values are clamped at init (never crashes boot). Default 0.0 keeps performance tracing off. |
| `OPENAI_MODEL` ⭐ **OpenAI Model Compatibility (2026-06-02)** | `"gpt-4.1-mini"` | **`gpt-4.1-mini`** (live, verified 2026-06-05 CRITICAL run) | Active OpenAI model name. `openai_service._build_completion_kwargs` automatically routes the request through the correct token-cap parameter: GPT-5.x / o1 / o3 / o4 families and anything containing `5.4` → `max_completion_tokens`; legacy (gpt-4.1-mini, gpt-4o, gpt-3.5-turbo) → `max_tokens`. Boot log `[openai] model=… token_param=…` confirms the active shape. Operator can flip the value in `.env` without code change. Sending BOTH `max_tokens` AND `max_completion_tokens` in one request also 400s — the builder selects EXACTLY ONE. |
| `BOOKING_CALENDAR_ID` ⭐ **Calendar Multi-Busy Check (2026-06-04)** | `""` | empty (live ⏳ not yet set) | The Google Calendar id where the agent CREATES consultation events. Empty → falls back to `GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ID`. The agent ONLY writes here (`book_slot` / `cancel_calendar_event` / `create_event`) — never any other busy-only calendar. Resolver: `settings.booking_calendar_id()`. |
| `BUSY_CALENDAR_IDS` ⭐ **Calendar Multi-Busy Check (2026-06-04)** | `""` | empty (live ⏳ not yet set) | Comma-separated list of Google Calendar ids the agent consults for availability via a single multi-item FreeBusy query. Empty → falls back to `[booking_calendar_id()]`. Booking calendar is ALWAYS included (deduped to the front). Each calendar MUST be shared with the service account or the FreeBusy response carries a per-calendar `errors` block → fail-CLOSED. Resolver: `settings.busy_calendar_ids()`. Live operator action pending — without the manager's side-calendar id here, the live bug the patch was authored against will recur. |
| `FOLLOWUP_ENABLED` ⭐ **Follow-up Test Mode Patch (2026-06-06)** | `True` | **`true`** (live) | Master kill switch for the follow-up channel. When False the scheduler tick is a single-line no-op (`[FOLLOWUP] disabled (FOLLOWUP_ENABLED=false) — tick skipped`). Independent of `FOLLOWUP_HOURS` and `AGENT_ENABLED`. |
| `FOLLOWUP_TEST_MODE` ⭐ **Follow-up Test Mode Patch (2026-06-06)** | `False` | **`true`** (live) | When True AND `FOLLOWUP_FIRST_DELAY_SECONDS > 0`, the FIRST follow-up cadence step (24h) reduces to that many seconds so operators can live-test the scheduler in ~2 minutes. Stages 2 (72h) and 3 (168h) are NEVER overridden. Test mode is *separate* from `FOLLOWUP_ENABLED` — disabling the master flag overrides the test mode. Tests pin OFF via `tests/conftest.py`. |
| `FOLLOWUP_FIRST_DELAY_SECONDS` ⭐ **Follow-up Test Mode Patch (2026-06-06)** | `0` | **`120`** (live) | Positive integer in seconds. When `FOLLOWUP_TEST_MODE=true` AND this is positive, the first-stage delay reduces to this many seconds. Zero / negative / non-numeric → silently ignored (production 24h cadence remains). |
| `META_APP_SECRET` (alias `MESSENGER_APP_SECRET`) ⭐ **Webhook Signature Verification** | `""` | (set on local; ⏳ to add on Railway) | Facebook Page app secret used by `_verify_meta_signature` for inbound `POST /webhook` HMAC-SHA256 verification. When empty AND `INSTAGRAM_APP_SECRET` is also empty AND `VERIFY_WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE=true`, the verifier fails-open with one warning per request. NEVER logged. |
| `INSTAGRAM_APP_SECRET` (alias `IG_APP_SECRET`) ⭐ **Instagram Webhook Signature (2026-06-09)** | `""` | (set on local; ⏳ to add on Railway) | Instagram Business app secret. The multi-secret verifier tries `META_APP_SECRET` first, falls back to `INSTAGRAM_APP_SECRET`. Meta serves Facebook Page webhooks and Instagram Business webhooks under SEPARATE app secrets, so this is required for IG signature verification to pass. LIVE VERIFIED 2026-06-09 — operator added local value, IG DM signature accepted. NEVER logged. |
| `INSTAGRAM_ACCESS_TOKEN` (alias `IG_ACCESS_TOKEN`) ⭐ **Instagram outbound DM (2026-06-09)** | `""` | (set on local; ⏳ to add on Railway) | Page-scoped Instagram access token used by `messenger_service.send_message(platform="instagram", ...)`. Boot log surfaces presence (`set` / `NOT set (outbound DM disabled)`). LIVE VERIFIED 2026-06-09 — agent responded to IG DM after operator added the token. NEVER logged. |
| `WHATSAPP_TOKEN` / `WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID` / `MANAGER_WHATSAPP_NUMBER` ⭐ **WhatsApp manager notification** | `""` / `""` / `""` | (⏳ pending real credentials) | When ANY is empty, `_send_manager_whatsapp` early-returns with a single `[NOTIFICATION][WHATSAPP] Skipped: missing credentials (...)` log line (Session 7 guard) — no `httpx.post`, no traceback. Email channel stays independent. Live test pending real Meta WhatsApp Business credentials. |
| `REDIS_URL` ⭐ **P3-B Redis Migration** | `""` | (set on local; ⏳ to add on Railway as Redis add-on URL) | Connection URL for Redis. Empty OR `REDIS_ENABLED=false` → app silently runs in-memory only (legacy mode). Conversations + manager-notified + processed-comment guards persisted with 7d sliding TTL when configured. Password never logged. |
| `GOOGLE_SHEETS_CREDENTIALS_JSON` / `GOOGLE_SHEETS_SPREADSHEET_ID` / `GOOGLE_SHEETS_LEADS_TAB` ⭐ **Google integrations** | (depends) | (set on local; ⏳ to add on Railway with client values) | Service-account credentials + spreadsheet id + tab name. The `events` subscriber tab (Session 11) is created on first write if missing. NEVER logged. The per-service `GOOGLE_*_CREDENTIALS_JSON` values are read from the `.env` file (local dev) and may be a path OR inline JSON. On Railway use `GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS_JSON` instead (below) — it is read from `os.environ` and wins. |
| `GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS_JSON` ⭐ **Railway-safe Google creds (2026-06-13)** | `""` | (⏳ set on Railway as the full service-account JSON) | The Railway-safe single source for BOTH Google Sheets AND Google Calendar service-account credentials. Read straight from `os.environ` by `app/services/google_credentials.py::load_google_credentials(scopes, file_value=...)` (Railway has no `.env` file, so this can't go through the `.env`-only `config._env`). When set → `json.loads` + escaped-`\n` repair on `private_key` + `service_account.Credentials.from_service_account_info(info, scopes=...)`. **Wins** over the per-service path / `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS`. Resolution order: `GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS_JSON` → per-service `file_value` (inline JSON or path) → `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` (file path) → clear secret-free error. NEVER logged; the "not configured" error contains NO credential contents. Used by `sheets_service._sheets_client()` (→ `gspread.authorize`) and `calendar_service._calendar_service()` (→ `build(..., credentials=...)`). |
| `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` ⭐ **Railway-safe Google creds (2026-06-13)** | `""` | (local fallback — path to credentials.json) | Standard Google credentials FILE-path env var. Read from `os.environ` by `load_google_credentials` as the file fallback when `GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS_JSON` is unset. `GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS_JSON` wins if both are set. |

App boot prints engine-flag values + Redis connection status + OpenAI model/token-param to stdout (`app/main.py` + `openai_service` module load). The new boot lines `🔐 webhook secrets: facebook_app_secret=set|NOT set instagram_app_secret=set|NOT set` and `🔐 instagram access token: set|NOT set (outbound DM disabled)` surface secret + token PRESENCE (never values).

## კონვენციები
- Python: type hints სავალდებულო, dataclass / pydantic Settings .env-ისთვის
- Async: ყველა I/O აპერაცია (Meta API, OpenAI, Sheets) async უნდა იყოს (with sync exceptions where caller chain is sync — see `openai_service.chat_with_tools`, `messenger_service.send_private_reply`)
- Error handling: try/except + logger.error/exception, არასოდეს silent fail
- Logs: `logger` (logging module) — არა `print()`
- Tests: pytest, mock external API calls

## ცოცხალი ტესტირება

**მოკლე test sweep (session-ის დასაწყისში):**
```
python test_agent.py                          # 68 mocked end-to-end checks ✅
pytest tests/                                 # 1840 passed, 0 failed today (baseline 1840 — 1350 base + 52 multi-event + 9 UI visibility + 31 ADULT Live QA + 23 ADULT price surfacing + 55 comment specific-event mapping + 73 ADULT event subscription + broadcast + 22 Instagram webhook signature + 25 generic-#event active-list + 28 client-smoke-regression + 27 live-polish-booking-wording + 22 P0 SC-06 ineligible-young + 33 live-smoke-followup booking-wording + 18 email-content-cleanup + 43 georgian-colloquial-time + 29 adult-event-date-filter; Guardrails patch rolled back 2026-06-09)
python tools/sim_followup.py --case all       # 8/8 follow-up scheduler scenarios (mocked Meta send)
python tools/sim_adult_flow.py --scenario all # 3/3 ADULT scenarios (mocked externals)
python tools/run_followup_tick.py --dry-run   # hydrate Redis + list due conversations, no send
```

### Live follow-up QA (2-minute test mode)

`.env`:
```
FOLLOWUP_ENABLED=true
FOLLOWUP_TEST_MODE=true
FOLLOWUP_FIRST_DELAY_SECONDS=120
```

With `FOLLOWUP_TEST_MODE=true` AND a positive `FOLLOWUP_FIRST_DELAY_SECONDS`, the FIRST follow-up cadence step (24h) is reduced to that many seconds so an operator can live-test the scheduler in about 2 minutes. Stages 2 (72h) and 3 (168h) are NEVER overridden — they always stay on production cadence.

Manual live-test steps:
1. `FLUSHALL` Redis (optional; preserve to test marker survival across restart).
2. Send a fresh Messenger DM. Agent asks a question.
3. Do NOT reply. Wait ≥ 120 seconds.
4. The next scheduler tick (hourly by default) emits the follow-up DM. **To trigger immediately from a one-off CLI**, use the hydrate helper so the fresh Python process can see the live server's Redis-persisted conversations:
   ```
   python tools/run_followup_tick.py            # hydrate + run tick
   python tools/run_followup_tick.py --dry-run  # hydrate + list due, no send
   ```
   `python -c "from app.services import followup_service; followup_service.check_and_send_followups()"` will NOT work on its own — the fresh process starts with an empty in-memory `conversation_service.conversations` dict and silently scans zero conversations.
5. Verify: one DM sent, log carries `[FOLLOWUP] Test mode enabled: first delay = 120s`, `[FOLLOWUP] scanning total=N parent=N with_marker=N`, `[FOLLOWUP] tick complete total=N due=1 sent=1 skipped=0`, `last_bot_message_at` is refreshed, `followup_stage` advanced to `first_24h`.
6. Second tick is a no-op (stage already advanced) — duplicate prevention.

Production cadence is unaffected: with `FOLLOWUP_TEST_MODE=false` or unset, the 24h/72h/168h schedule remains exactly as before. Invalid `FOLLOWUP_FIRST_DELAY_SECONDS` (zero / negative / non-numeric) silently falls back to production cadence — the scheduler never crashes.

**Production checklist for follow-up:** set `FOLLOWUP_TEST_MODE=false` (or remove the line) AND remove `FOLLOWUP_FIRST_DELAY_SECONDS` before Railway deploy. The override applies only to the FIRST cadence step (24h); stages 2 (72h) + 3 (168h) are NEVER overridden by code design, but the safest production posture is to disable test mode entirely. The APScheduler tick interval stays at 1h regardless of mode.

## ცნობილი პრობლემები

**CRITICAL — Railway deploy blockers (from the Deploy-Readiness Audit, 2026-06-13):**
- ⏳ **`config._env` reads the `.env` FILE ONLY** ([app/config.py](app/config.py) line 14/31-33, `ENV_VALUES = dotenv_values(.env)`) → Railway dashboard env vars (`os.environ`) are INVISIBLE, and `settings = get_settings()` raises `ConfigurationError` on the 7 required vars at import → **app crashes on boot on Railway**. Fix: `_env` must read `os.environ` first, then `.env` fallback. (Only `GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS_JSON` already reads `os.environ`.) `REDIS_URL` shares this bug → Redis invisible on Railway until fixed.
- ⏳ **`requirements.txt` missing runtime deps:** `redis` (persistence), `tzdata` (zoneinfo boot-safety on slim images), `python-multipart` (Admin Panel form POSTs). Versions unpinned (breaking-update risk).

- ⏳ **Railway runtime:** single replica / one uvicorn worker (else duplicate APScheduler → duplicate follow-up DMs); always-on (no scale-to-zero). Procfile + `app.main:app` + `$PORT` are already correct. See HANDOFF „RAILWAY DEPLOY BLOCKERS".

- ✅ ~~NEXT TASK — PARENT contact-capture (4 bugs, per-bug pytest gate ≥2086). Trace BUG 1 before fixing.~~ — RESOLVED 2026-06-12 by **PARENT Contact-Capture (BUG 1–4)** (+34 tests, 2086 → 2120; CRITICAL 22/22; `test_agent.py` green). **BUG 1 trace DISPROVED the parser-regression hypothesis:** `_parse_name_phone("595999733")` → `("", "595999733")` extracts the bare phone correctly — there is NO deterministic rejection. The gap was that the only deterministic contact capture (`_capture_contact_and_ask_time`) is gated behind an ACTIVE booking sub-flow, so a no-`pending_booking` contact turn fell through to the stochastic LLM (which has no phone fallback); „595999733 ეს არის ნომერი" gave the LLM a „this is the number" cue while bare „595999733" was ambiguous. (1+2) new `parent_flow._maybe_handle_contact_collection` (engine path, before LLM/commit) captures a contact-only phone (+ any-order name) and replies deterministically — reversed „595999733 ლიზი" is contact, never booking/time; a future bookable confirmed slot still books via the commit helper (`_pending_iso_is_future_bookable`). (3) `parent_llm_engine._is_known_about_you_preamble` (in `_strip_concern_wording`) strips „თქვენი ასაკი/სახელი/ინფორმაცია უკვე ვიცი" (the „თქვენი" anchor preserves „სახელი უკვე ვიცი" + the privacy notice + booking confirmations). (4) `parent_flow._maybe_request_full_contact_on_intent` asks the COMPLETE name+phone on an explicit „კი მინდა"/„ჩამწერეთ" for an eligible lead with contact missing. Generic + state-based, no hardcoded users; no Calendar/Sheets-schema/adult/broadcast/email/model/prompt change. See HANDOFF / REVIEW_PACK „Contact-Capture".

- ✅ ~~NEXT TASK — Name/Phone parser corrupts the name when a date/time is in the same message; contact-only books a stale time; challenge Sheet column polluted.~~ — RESOLVED 2026-06-11 by **PARENT Contact Extraction + Booking State + Challenge Cleanup** (+54 tests, 1967 → 2021). (1) `parent_flow._parse_name_phone` rejects month/date/time/booking + digit-bearing tokens as name candidates (new `_name_token_is_valid`, reusing `GEORGIAN_MONTH_STEMS`); public `is_valid_person_name` guards every save chokepoint (`_save_lead_info`, `_book_consultation`, engine pending-commit, legacy router). (2) `_maybe_commit_pending_booking_engine` never auto-books a STALE/past pending datetime — `_pending_iso_is_stale` + `_clear_stale_pending_datetime` + `_capture_contact_and_ask_time` save the contact and ask for a fresh time (kills the „16:45 წარსული დროა" bug); a FUTURE confirmed slot still books on contact/„კი". (3) `_sanitise_invalid_stored_name` clears an invalid stored name before the engine claims „სახელი უკვე ვიცი"; audit confirmed adult-subscription rows never hydrate PARENT contact. (4) `parent_llm_engine.clean_challenge_for_storage` strips a tacked-on factual question at the SAVE chokepoint (`_save_lead_info` + `maybe_capture_challenge_fallback`) so the Sheets challenge column is clean. Generic + state-based, no hardcoded users. PARENT-only; no Calendar/Sheets-schema/adult/comment/email/model/prompt change.

- ✅ ~~Child-age extraction read „9-17" (camp age band / menu) as the child's age → agent skipped asking the real age.~~ — RESOLVED 2026-06-11 by **State Consistency + Child-Age Extraction** (+52 tests, 1915 → 1967; incl. adversarial-review fix for the „N-ზე" colloquial-hour leak). `parent_llm_engine.maybe_capture_child_age_fallback` now rejects ranges (`_contains_age_range`), rejects time/date numbers (`_number_is_time_or_date`), and requires age context (წლ/წელ word, child word, or `age_question_pending` — a bare standalone number is read as age ONLY when the bot just asked). `parent_tool_executor._save_lead_info` rejects range child_age args. `parent_flow._ensure_camp_age_question` (FIX 2) guarantees a new PARENT user with unknown child_age is asked the age; `_maybe_requalify_child` + `_maybe_acknowledge_stored_state` (FIX 3) add resume transparency + re-qualification. - ✅ ~~NEXT TASK — ADULT subscription confirms but does NOT save.~~ — RESOLVED 2026-06-11 by **ADULT Subscription Confirmation + Write** (+42 tests incl. 6 adversarial-review regressions, 1873 → 1915). Root cause: `is_subscription_consent_phrase` was defined but wired NOWHERE (its wiring was removed in the rolled-back Guardrails patch) and no pending-offer state was ever recorded, so subscription depended entirely on the stochastic LLM calling the tool — which it missed. New deterministic layer in `adult_llm_engine.run_adult_llm_turn` (before the off-topic guard + LLM): `_has_pending_subscription_offer` (status `"asked"` OR last assistant turn was the offer), `_is_subscription_consent` (pending-offer + token-boundary so „კი" inside „კიდევ" never matches), `_is_direct_subscription_intent`, `_deterministic_subscribe` (writes via `AdultToolExecutor`, confirms only after a written row, honest already/missing/failed branches), `_mark_subscription_offer_if_present` (sets `"asked"` when the agent asks the offer), plus a sanitiser safety net `_strip_false_subscription_success` (strips invented „ჩაგწერეთ სიაში"/„უკვე ხართ სიაში" when no confirmed write this turn). New per-turn executor flag `adult_subscription_confirmed_for_conversation`. Subscription never calls `broadcast_event`; `LIVE_BROADCAST_ENABLED=false` stays safe. ADULT prompt NOT changed (no broad prompt polish before the production smoke). See HANDOFF "ADULT Subscription Confirmation + Write Patch".

- ✅ ~~PARENT booking flow regression (client smoke): "კი მინდა" → manager callback, not booking; "595999733 ტესტ" rejected; "კომპიუტერის მეხსიერების" phrase.~~ — RESOLVED 2026-06-09 (+28 tests, 1640 → 1668). System prompt CRITICAL blocks + sanitiser fix.
- ✅ ~~Google Sheet append not working with client credentials.~~ — VERIFIED WORKING 2026-06-09. `create_lead()` live verified.
- ✅ ~~Google Calendar create not working with client credentials.~~ — VERIFIED WORKING 2026-06-09. `create_event()` + `cancel_calendar_event()` live verified.
- ⏳ **Conversation guardrails redesign** — Guardrails patch (scolding-phrase ban + manager-phone shortcut + low-value guard) was rolled back 2026-06-09 due to PARENT booking regression. Needs redesign with narrower, tested scope. Postponed until after client production setup + smoke test.
- ⏳ **Token-waste control** — Low-value message bypass (კაი / მადლობა / emojis) postponed. To be implemented as isolated, heavily-tested patch after production baseline confirmed.
- ⏳ **Manager handoff wording** — Direct phone return (no LLM) for explicit manager requests: needs narrower implementation that does not affect booking flow. Postponed.
- ⏳ **Adult subscriber vs specific event clarification** — Prompt-only clarification block for `subscribe_to_adult_event_updates` vs registration. Can be added as isolated prompt patch after production setup.

**Priority 0 — SECURITY / config hardening before any deploy (operator action, 2026-06-12):**
0a. **REGENERATE the Meta access token** — a token leaked in a terminal error log during testing; rotate it before production.
0b. **Make Google credentials Railway-safe** — ✅ CODE DONE (2026-06-13, Railway-Safe Google Creds): both Sheets + Calendar load via `app/services/google_credentials.py::load_google_credentials`; set the Railway env var `GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS_JSON=<full service-account JSON>` (read from `os.environ`, wins over the file path; escaped `\n` in `private_key` auto-repaired). Local dev keeps `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS=path/to/credentials.json` (or the per-service `GOOGLE_*_CREDENTIALS_JSON`). **Operator action remaining:** paste the JSON into the Railway variable; do NOT commit `credentials.json`. `runtime.txt` pins `python-3.11`.
0c. **Deactivate the test adult events** (`ჯონი` / `formula_1` / `summer_fest` in `data/admin_config/sections.yaml`) before client launch — dirty data, NOT a code bug (the active+future filter works).
0d. **Deploy to STAGING first** (a test Page, not the client's live Page) before flipping to the client Page.

**Priority 1 — client production setup (operator action):**
1. Create / switch to client Google Sheet (replace tester Sheet ID in `.env`).
2. Create / switch to client Google Calendar (replace tester Calendar ID in `.env`).
3. Configure client email notifications (`MANAGER_EMAIL`, SMTP settings).
4. Configure WhatsApp manager notification if token is now available.
5. Connect client Facebook Page + Instagram Business account to the Meta App.
6. Update local `.env` with all client resource IDs.
7. Run full local smoke test with client config (`python test_agent.py` + manual DM test on each platform).

**Priority 2 — deployment:**
8. Railway deploy (Procfile + always-on plan + Redis add-on).
9. Add all Railway env variables (same set as local `.env`, see HANDOFF §7 / REVIEW_PACK STILL OPEN list).
10. Update Meta webhook URL to Railway public URL.
11. Run Railway smoke test.

**Priority 3 — post-deploy:**
12. Meta App Review submission (`pages_manage_engagement`).
13. WhatsApp live test once real credentials issued.
14. Adult follow-up scheduler (`followup_service` extension to `segment == "ADULT"`).
15. SC-14 / SC-23 / SC-42 NORMAL/IMPORTANT regressions — confirm or fix.
16. Dead code cleanup / `.gitignore` / `requirements-dev` / v18→v19 / `mask_sender` quick-fixes (~1 hour).

**Remaining open edge risk (accepted):**
- Manager phone pre-LLM handler does NOT invoke `request_manager_callback` on the first explicit-manager-request turn (no Sheets write / email notification on that turn). Follow-up turns with contact info can still call the tool normally.
- Low-value guard edge: `"კარგი"` after a bot statement with no `?` is treated as low-value even if user meant "OK, proceed". `?`-gate covers the most common actionable cases.

**⏳ Railway / production env setup — operator action, NOT a local engineering blocker:**
- Production `.env` on Railway must include: `INSTAGRAM_APP_SECRET`, `INSTAGRAM_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `META_APP_SECRET` (Facebook Page secret), Google credentials (`GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS_JSON` / `GOOGLE_SHEETS_SPREADSHEET_ID` / `BOOKING_CALENDAR_ID` / `BUSY_CALENDAR_IDS`), `REDIS_URL`, `WHATSAPP_TOKEN` + `WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID` + `MANAGER_WHATSAPP_NUMBER` (when WhatsApp credentials issued).
- Railway deploy itself is pending. Meta App Review pending. Client production setup pending.

**RESOLVED — Conversation Guardrails + Token Waste Control + Adult Event Registration Clarification (2026-06-09, +87 tests, 1640 → 1727):**
- ✅ ~~Scolding / defensive phrases ("უკანასკნელად გითხარით", "როგორც უკვე გითხარით", "ამაზე უკვე გიპასუხეთ" + 12 variants)~~ → 15 entries added to `FORBIDDEN_PHRASE_REPLACEMENTS` (PARENT) and `ADULT_FORBIDDEN_PHRASE_REPLACEMENTS` (ADULT); stripped to `""`. Surrounding sentence context preserved.
- ✅ ~~Agent hedged with "თუ გსურთ, დაგაკავშირებთ" after user already explicitly asked for manager~~ → new `_is_explicit_manager_request()` / `_parent_is_explicit_manager_request()` (15 phrases each) pre-LLM handler in both engines returns `"მენეჯერი: {phone}"` directly — manager phone from `admin_config_service.get_manager_phone()` (fallback `558 67 47 33`). No OpenAI call on that first turn. Follow-up contact info still routes through the manager-callback tool normally.
- ✅ ~~Adult event subscriber list falsely implied specific event registration~~ → "სუბსქრიფციის vs კონკრეტულ ღონისძიებაზე რეგისტრაციის განსხვავება — CRITICAL" block added to `system_adult_v1.md`. Agent must clarify that subscription = future notifications only; provide link or manager phone (`558 67 47 33`) for actual registration.
- ✅ ~~OpenAI tokens wasted on "კაი", "მადლობა", emojis, repeated messages~~ → deterministic low-value guard in both engines; canned ack without LLM call. Safety valve: guard skips when last bot turn had `?` (consent / slot questions); PARENT also skips when `pending_booking` is set.
- ✅ ~~"კი" in "კიდევ ერთი კითხვა მაქვს" triggered adult subscription consent~~ → `_consent_phrase_matches()` applies word-boundary check for short standalone tokens ("კი", "ok", "yes"). Standalone tokens match; substring-within-word does not. Multi-word phrases unchanged.

**RESOLVED — Adult Event Date Filter: hide past events from users (2026-06-10, +29 tests, 1811 → 1840):**
- ✅ ~~Past adult events shown when active=true (no date comparison)~~ → `get_active_adult_events` now excludes past + non-empty-unparseable-date events by default (chokepoint for adult DM list, generic `#event` comment list, specific resolver, number selection). `is_adult_event_past` is granularity-aware (time / date / month). Parser supports „DD <month> [HH:MM]" / „<month>" / „2026 წლის ივლისი" (stem-matched, typo-tolerant; explicit year honoured; else current-year w/ >180d Dec→Jan rollover).
- ✅ Specific past-event reference (comment tag / fb_post_id / title selection) → ended-event message („ეს ღონისძიება უკვე დასრულებულია…"), never the ticket link. `resolve_specific_adult_event` reason `past_event`; executor `_get_adult_event_details` reason `event_past`; prompt rule added.
- ✅ Broadcast (manual / after-save / subscription) blocks past events with `reason="event_past"` + operator-facing Admin Panel message.
- ✅ Empty date_text still shows (operator omitted deliberately); Admin Panel still lists past events internally. NOT a Calendar issue — `calendar_service.py` untouched. PARENT/Sheets/email/webhook/OpenAI-model untouched.

**RESOLVED — Georgian Colloquial Time: deterministic PM normalization (2026-06-10, +43 tests, 1768 → 1811):**
- ✅ ~~Same colloquial hour interpreted differently across users/states; typo „სათზე" unsupported; engine path left the hour to the stochastic LLM~~ → new `timestamps.extract_colloquial_hour` + `apply_colloquial_time_to_iso` (single source of truth). PARENT booking rule: unqualified 1–9 → +12 (13:00–21:00); 10/11/12 literal; „დილ…" morning literal; „საღამო…" evening 1–11 → +12; explicit HH:MM literal. Typo-tolerant: საათზე / სათზე / საათი / სათი / საათისთვის / სთ / სთ-ზე / 8-ზე / 8ზე. Bare hour trusted only with a morning/evening qualifier (guarded vs „N წლის").
- ✅ Wired into `parent_tool_executor._normalise_datetime_iso_from_message` (chokepoint for check_consultation_slot + book_consultation + reschedule) AND the legacy `parent_turn_router._parse_booking_datetime` (compound-booking fallback) — identical behaviour everywhere, all contact/booking states.
- ✅ NOT a Calendar issue — `calendar_service.py` untouched; the hour reaching the (correct) FreeBusy check was wrong. Prompt NOT cleaned (time rules cover more than colloquial PM; LLM still composes spoken reply — removing guidance risks spoken/checked mismatch).

**RESOLVED — Email Content Cleanup: manager email challenge/summary (2026-06-10, +18 tests, 1750 → 1768):**
- ✅ ~~Raw chat/filler leaked into „ინტერესი / გამოწვევა" + duplicated in „მოკლე რეზიუმე"~~ → `notification_service._clean_challenge_for_email` splits the raw challenge on commas/„ასევე", drops factual-question clauses (`_EMAIL_QUESTION_STEMS`), strips filler (`_EMAIL_CHALLENGE_FILLER`: ასევე მაინტერესებს / მაინტერესებს / კი მინდა / ჩაწერა მინდა / კონსულტაცია მინდა / დეტალები / მომწერეთ / პირობებში რა იგულისხმება / …), canonicalises ეკრან-variants → „ეკრანთან დროის შემცირება", dedupes. `_build_parent_summary` uses the cleaned goals.
- ✅ ~~Factual question mixed into goals~~ → `_extract_additional_question` surfaces it on a separate optional „დამატებითი კითხვა:" line; never in the goals field.
- ✅ ~~Unknown challenge invented/omitted~~ → empty cleaned challenge prints the explicit „არ არის მითითებული" placeholder; never invented.
- ✅ `lead.challenge` is NOT mutated — Sheets/CRM write unaffected. Email-rendering only; no Calendar / Sheets schema / adult / comment / webhook / OpenAI-model / SMTP / booking-flow change. Existing inline „label: value" layout preserved (29 wording tests still pass).

**RESOLVED — Live Smoke Followup: PARENT booking confirmation wording (2026-06-10, +33 tests, 1717 → 1750):**
- ✅ ~~Confirmation + extra question in one message re-asked for confirmation~~ → `_user_confirmed_booking` (parent_llm_engine) now matches a strong confirmation phrase at the start of the leading clause (`_STRONG_CONFIRM_LEAD_PHRASES`) with a negation guard (`_CONFIRM_NEGATION_TOKENS`), in addition to whole-message exact match. `_BOOKING_OFFER_STEMS` extended with „ჩავნიშნავ"/„დამიდასტურეთ"/„თავისუფალია" so `_last_bot_offered_booking` recognises the real brand offer. The existing „proceed directly to booking, don't re-ask" sales-context hint now fires for „კი მაწყობს ეს დრო, …?".
- ✅ ~~„მადლობა თქვენ" on a non-thanks booking confirmation~~ → `parent_flow._strip_unwarranted_thanks_in_booking_confirmation` strips the leading thanks opener from a booking-confirmation response when the user's message has no thanks token; preserved when the user actually thanked.
- ✅ ~~Goal/challenge capture felt weak~~ → eligible-age sales-context hint asks ONE clean goal question when `lead.challenge` is empty and explicitly never blocks an explicit booking; once known it stops re-asking. Per-turn context hint only — system prompt untouched.
- ✅ Busy-slot rejection **confirmed live** (11 ივნისს 16:00 → unavailable; 18:00 → offered). Calendar service NOT modified.

**RESOLVED — P0 Stabilization: SC-06 ineligible-young (2026-06-09, +22 tests, 1695 → 1717):**
- ✅ ~~CRITICAL SC-06 "Ineligible Age — 8 წლის" flaky (~40% pass); audit found CRITICAL silently at 21/22 while docs claimed 22/22~~ → root cause: `_strip_consultation_cta_if_ineligible` only appended the manager-handoff line (carrying both "ასაკი" and "მენეჯერ") WHEN a booking CTA was present; a CTA-free-but-vague LLM reply for a sub-minimum-age child slipped through, intermittently failing `("9" OR "ასაკი") AND "მენეჯერ"`. New `parent_flow._ensure_ineligible_young_age_message(conversation, message, response)` (wired right after the CTA scrubber) deterministically replaces the reply on the **disclosure turn** when `age < age_min` with: „ბანაკში მონაწილეობა შესაძლებელია 9–17 წლის ბავშვებისთვის. ამ ასაკისთვის ბანაკში ჩაწერას ვერ შემოგთავაზებთ. თუ გსურთ, მენეჯერთან დაგაკავშირებთ და დამატებით ინფორმაციას მოგაწვდიან." Disclosure-turn guard (current message must carry the same sub-min age) prevents repeating on later thank-you turns. SC-06 isolated **5/5**; CRITICAL **22/22** clean.
- ✅ Scope guard: **over-age 18+ untouched** (SC-07 unaffected — handled by adult-switch / over-17 wording); eligible **9–17 pass through**; `age_min`/`age_max` read from `camp_2026.yaml` (data-driven, not hard-coded).

**Calendar availability — audit finding (2026-06-09, no code change):** the Calendar backend is **WORKING and live-verified**. A read-only FreeBusy diagnostic against the client calendar correctly flagged a real busy block (2026-06-11 13:15–17:45): 16:00 / 17:00 → unavailable, 18:00 → available, 12:00 → available (does not overlap), 16:30 → `half_hour_not_supported`. `GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ID` == `BOOKING_CALENDAR_ID` == `BUSY_CALENDAR_IDS` (same client calendar). Service account reads the calendar (no fail-closed), conflict logic + timezone correct. **Double protection:** `check_consultation_slot` AND `book_consultation` both call FreeBusy — a busy slot cannot be booked. The reported "busy slot still bookable" issue did NOT reproduce at the backend level; the only remaining unknown is live LLM tool-call discipline (calling `check_consultation_slot` before saying "თავისუფალია") — to be confirmed by the live smoke. `calendar_service.py` is NOT modified.

**Live booking-conflict smoke (operator, run before any production go):**
1. Clear Redis (`FLUSHALL`); restart server.
2. In Messenger/Instagram send, one per turn: `გამარჯობა` → `საზაფხულო ბანაკი` → `14 წლის არის` → `კონსულტაციის ჩაწერა მინდა` → `595999733` → `16 საათზე მინდა`. (Use a date with a known busy block, e.g. the live 2026-06-11 13:15–17:45.)
   - **Expected:** agent must NOT confirm 16:00; it must say the time is unavailable and offer alternatives. **If it confirms 16:00 → STOP, do not deploy.**
3. Then send `18 საათზე მინდა`. **Expected:** if backend confirms free, agent offers/confirms 18:00; on user confirm → Calendar event created + Sheet row appended.

**RESOLVED — Generic `#event` Comment → Active Adult Events List (2026-06-09, +25 tests, 1615 → 1640):**
- ✅ ~~A comment of „ფასი?" / „სად ტარდება?" / „მაინტერესებს" / „ბმული?" under a generic `#event` post produced the misleading „ახლო მომავალში ღონისძიებების განრიგს გამოვაქვეყნებთ…" copy even though `adult_events.events[]` had active events.~~ → new `comment_service._build_active_adult_events_list_dm()` reads active events via `admin_config_service.get_active_adult_events()`, renders each one (title / date / location / price / link via the existing ADULT price rule), and substitutes the rendered block into the operator-editable `adult_events_comment_dm` template's `{events_list}` placeholder. `send_dm_from_comment` prefers the list helper over `_build_adult_rich_dm()` on BOTH the admin-section path (`section.type == "adult_events"`) AND the legacy hashtag-fallback path. When NO active events exist, the existing „ახლო მომავალში…" fallback fires (the patch deliberately does NOT promise events that don't exist).
- ✅ ~~Specific-event tag priority could be lost behind the new generic list~~ → Priority A (`facebook_post_id`) / B (event tag in comment) / C (event tag in caption) all run BEFORE the new list path in `send_dm_from_comment`. `#event #fast` still sends the exact „fast"-tagged event; `#event` alone sends the active-events catalogue.
- ✅ ~~`adult_events_comment_dm` template's `{events_list}` placeholder was declared but never populated~~ → builder substitutes the rendered events block. Defensive guard: if the template is missing OR rendering returns empty OR leaves a literal `{events_list}` behind OR somehow drops the events block (operator removed the placeholder), the builder falls back to a hard-coded Georgian frame. The literal `{events_list}` string NEVER surfaces to the user.
- ✅ ~~Instagram vs Facebook divergence~~ → the new path is platform-neutral. Integration tests cover both Instagram and Facebook private-reply routing for generic `#event`.
- ✅ ~~Camp routing risk~~ → `#ბანაკი` / `#camp` paths are unchanged — they still route through `_build_parent_rich_dm()`. Regression test asserts adult-event titles do not leak into camp DMs.

**RESOLVED — Instagram Webhook Signature + Payload Diagnostic (2026-06-09, +22 tests, 1593 → 1615):**
- ✅ **Instagram local/live DM response WORKS after operator env setup (LIVE VERIFIED 2026-06-09).** Operator added `INSTAGRAM_APP_SECRET` + `INSTAGRAM_ACCESS_TOKEN` to local `.env`, restarted server, sent IG DM, observed `[webhook] signature accepted via instagram_app_secret` (no 403). Agent responded successfully. Comment under Instagram post also passes signature.
- ✅ ~~Instagram webhook POSTs rejected with 403 / `[webhook] signature rejected`~~ → `_verify_meta_signature` now multi-secret. Tries Facebook (`META_APP_SECRET` / `MESSENGER_APP_SECRET`) first, falls back to `INSTAGRAM_APP_SECRET`. Returns `(accepted, label)` where label is a privacy-safe id (`facebook_app_secret` / `instagram_app_secret`) for the matched secret. Receive-webhook log now emits `[webhook] signature accepted via <label>` / `[webhook] signature rejected: no configured secret matched`. Constant-time `hmac.compare_digest` per candidate. Existing Facebook signature path and tests preserved byte-for-byte.
- ✅ ~~No safe post-acceptance diagnostic for Instagram payloads~~ → new `_summarise_payload_fields(payload)` returns `{object, entries, fields, supported}` for a single log line: `[webhook] accepted object=instagram entries=N fields=['messaging'|'messages'|'changes'|...]`. Unsupported fields produce `[webhook] instagram payload accepted but unsupported fields=[...] — handler will no-op` + 200 OK so Meta does not retry-storm. NEVER logs message text / sender ids / access tokens / raw body / signature header / app secrets.
- ✅ ~~No boot-time visibility into Instagram credentials~~ → `app/main.py` startup now prints `🔐 webhook secrets: facebook_app_secret=set|NOT set instagram_app_secret=set|NOT set` and `🔐 instagram access token: set|NOT set (outbound DM disabled)`. Presence only — never the values.
- ✅ ~~`Settings` had no Instagram fields~~ → new `INSTAGRAM_APP_SECRET: str = ""` and `INSTAGRAM_ACCESS_TOKEN: str = ""` with `from_env` readers (also accept `IG_APP_SECRET` / `IG_ACCESS_TOKEN` aliases). Existing Facebook config untouched.

**RESOLVED — Adult Event Subscription + New-Event Broadcast (2026-06-08, +73 tests, 1520 → 1593):**
- ✅ ~~No adult/cultural-event subscription / broadcast surface~~ → new `events` Sheets tab (18 columns), new `adult_subscription_service` (consent/unsubscribe phrase detection + subscribe/unsubscribe orchestration + already-subscribed lookup), new `adult_event_broadcast_service` (operator-triggered fan-out with kill-switch + missing-link + inactive blocked branches + per-subscriber failure isolation + dual-layer duplicate prevention via `Notified Event IDs`). New ADULT tool `subscribe_to_adult_event_updates` with executor handler that pulls name/phone from args first, falls back to lead state, never claims success without Sheets `(True, "ok")`. New `Conversation.adult_subscription_status` field round-trips through `to_dict`/`from_dict`.
- ✅ ~~No deterministic unsubscribe path~~ → `adult_llm_engine.run_adult_llm_turn` short-circuits on `is_unsubscribe_phrase(...)` BEFORE the LLM; calls `adult_subscription_service.unsubscribe(...)` and returns the canned confirmation directly. Not-subscribed → soft message. Sheets failure → manager-handoff offer.
- ✅ ~~No Admin Panel surface for subscription broadcasts~~ → checkbox „შენახვის შემდეგ გაუგზავნე subscribed მომხმარებლებს" on the create/edit event form (default off); per-row "გაგზავნა subscribed მომხმარებლებთან" manual broadcast button on the events list; results page with sent / skipped / failed counts in operator-friendly Georgian with explicit reasons (missing link / inactive event / no subscribers / kill switch).
- ✅ ~~No prompt rule for the subscription consent question~~ → `system_adult_v1.md` gains the „ფუტურული ღონისძიების შეტყობინებების წესი" CRITICAL block: exact brand wording, ban on misleading „ახალი ღონისძიების სიაში დაგამატოთ", explicit branches for missing name / phone / both, ban on incorrect „დაგიმატეთ".
- ✅ ~~No regression on PARENT booking / Calendar / Sheets Leads tab / OpenAI model / scenario runner / Comment routing / Admin Panel auth / WhatsApp manager notification~~ — none of these are touched. The 1520-baseline tests continue to pass.

**RESOLVED — Comment → Specific Event Mapping (2026-06-08, +55 tests, 1465 → 1520):**
- ✅ ~~Comment intent classifier relied on the LLM for obvious interest phrases~~ → new deterministic `comment_service.is_interest_intent(comment_text)` matches the closed set of Georgian + English broad-interest keywords („ფასი", „ბმული", „სად ტარდება", „price", „link", etc. — 30+ phrases). Called BEFORE the LLM in `handle_comment`; LLM is only consulted when no keyword match.
- ✅ ~~Comments under event-specific posts received only the generic adult-events DM~~ → new `comment_service.resolve_specific_adult_event(comment_text, post_id, platform)` returns `(event, candidates, reason)` with priority A (`facebook_post_id` exact match) → B (event tag in comment) → C (event tag in post caption) → D (no_match) → E (ambiguous when 2+ events match). Inactive events excluded from every priority. `send_dm_from_comment` tries the specific-event branch BEFORE falling through to the existing generic ADULT DM.
- ✅ ~~No specific-event DM builder~~ → new `_build_specific_adult_event_dm(event)` renders title / date / location / price / description / link with the existing ADULT price rules (numeric `price_text` → „<n> ლარი"; `price_gel` fallback when text blank). Sold-out banner ONLY when operator flagged. Missing link → manager-handoff line. Missing field → skipped (no fabricated copy).
- ✅ ~~Post-caption fetch leaked access token in error logs~~ → `fetch_post_content` hardened: log lines emit only the status code + masked post id + exception class name. Token, params dict, response body, and exception args are NEVER logged. Soft-fails (returns `""`) on 4xx / 5xx / exception so the webhook never blocks on Meta downtime.
- ✅ ~~Multi-match silently picked one event~~ → new `_build_ambiguous_adult_event_dm(candidates)` lists candidate titles and asks „რომელი გაინტერესებთ?".
- ✅ ~~Admin Panel event form had no helper text for tags / fb_post_id~~ → `templates/admin/adult_event_form.html` gains operator-facing helper text under both inputs explaining the optional-field semantics and example tag values.
- ✅ ~~Camp comment broad-interest routing also limited to „მაინტერესებს"~~ → The deterministic `is_interest_intent` shortcut benefits camp comments equally — „ფასი?" / „სად ტარდება?" / „ბმული?" / „ასაკი?" / „პირობები?" under #ბანაკი now route through the existing camp DM via the keyword shortcut, NOT through the stochastic LLM. No new camp-specific code needed — same closed-set keyword list serves both flows.

- ✅ ~~Agent said „დასწრების საფასური ღონისძიების კონფიგურაციაში მითითებული არაა." while Admin Panel had `price_text="150"` and `price_gel=150`~~ → `_get_adult_events` compact + `_get_adult_event_details` now both surface `price_gel` alongside the existing `price_text` (positive integers only — zero is omitted). **RESOLVED — ADULT Live QA Patch — sold-out hallucination + ticket link + partial title + wording polish (2026-06-08, +31 tests, 1411 → 1442):**
- ✅ ~~Agent invented „ადგილები ამოწურულია" with no operator signal~~ → `_normalize_adult_event` surfaces an explicit `sold_out` boolean (default False). `_get_adult_events` compact OMITS `seats_available` when zero/unset so the LLM cannot read „0" as „empty". Sanitiser strips invented sold-out copy (sentence-level removal) UNLESS the executor flagged a sold-out disclosure for this turn (new `adult_sold_out_disclosed_for_conversation` flag). - ✅ ~~Partial title matching failed („ქართული პოეზია" did not match „ქართული პოეზიის საღამო")~~ → new `_GEORGIAN_NOUN_SUFFIXES` + `_stem_token_for_match` + stem-aware token-overlap matcher. `find_adult_events_matching(needle)` returns ALL candidates; `find_adult_event` returns the unique match or None. Exact id > exact title > substring (≥3 char guard) > stem overlap.

- ✅ ~~Ambiguous selection silently picked one event~~ → multi-match returns `success=false, reason="ambiguous_event", candidates=[…]`. Inactive-only match returns `reason="event_inactive", matched_titles=[…]` (distinct from `unknown_event`). Prompt rule handles all four branches explicitly.
- ✅ ~~Filler „გმადლობთ. რამდენი წლის ბრძანდებით?"~~ → `_ADULT_LEADING_THANKS_PATTERNS` strips the sentence-initial thanks opener before the adult age question. Mid-response „გმადლობთ" is preserved (legitimate thanks).
- ✅ ~~„გინდა?" / „გინდათ?" copy in adult flow~~ → `_ADULT_WORDING_REWRITES` rewrites to „გსურთ?" (Bug 5). Polite-form rule added to `system_adult_v1.md`.
- ✅ ~~Blanket „ორივე 13+ წლისთვისაა" line on event listing~~ → prompt rule: min_age explicit only when user age is near the threshold; otherwise list focuses on title / date / location / price.

**RESOLVED — Session 8 (2026-06-07 LIVE QA PATCH, +17 tests, 1333 → 1350) ✅ LIVE VERIFIED:**

New `_DUP_TU_MIXED_PATTERN` collapses doubled mixed-verb clauses. **LIVE VERIFIED:** booking confirmation reads cleanly as „[date], [time] საათზე კონსულტაცია ჩაგინიშნეთ. მენეჯერი დაგიკავშირდებათ.".

Sheets failure: warning logged with masked sender + masked old_event_id, Sentry capture, Calendar success NOT rolled back. **LIVE VERIFIED:** operator screenshot confirmed old row → `"Rescheduled"`, current row → `"Booked"`; exactly one active `"Booked"` row per sender.

**RESOLVED — ADULT live-bug trio (2026-06-02, ADULT Context Routing Fix):**
- ✅ ~~ADULT transition still stops in live.~~ — `_ensure_adult_intro_followup` now has a broader heuristic (`_looks_like_bare_intro`) that catches short ack openers (`გასაგებია` / `კარგი` / `მშვენიერია` / `კარგით` / `მადლობა`) paired with adult-event keywords (`ღონისძიებ` / `საღამო` / `კულტურულ` / `ზრდასრულ`) when the response has no `?` and ≤120 chars. Follow-up question branches: `adult_target_age` → offer-list; `adult_target_relation` only → ask „თქვენი {relation} რამდენი წლისაა?"; `adult_age` → offer-list; nothing → who-question.
- ✅ ~~`child_age` is leaking into ADULT context as the user's age.~~ — `_get_adult_events` priority is now `user_age` arg → `adult_target_age` → `adult_age`; `child_age` is NEVER a fallback. Deterministic guard blocks the LLM from passing `user_age=<child_age>` when no relative target is on record. New `lead.adult_target_age` and `lead.adult_target_relation` fields capture the relative the event is for.
- ✅ ~~„დის(თვის)" / „ძმის(თვის)" wrongly triggers PARENT switch.~~ — `_user_wants_parent_flow` now requires an explicit *hard camp keyword* (`ბანაკ` / `საზაფხულო` / `ბავშვთა პროგრამა`). Soft cues paired with an adult-event signal stay ADULT. Relative cues like „ჩემი დისთვის" / „ძმისთვის" / „მეგობრისთვის" never trigger the switch alone. New `_maybe_capture_adult_target` pre-LLM helper records the relation + age on the lead.

Per-event min_age can override UPWARD only. „min_age: 10" or missing min_age → 13. `system_adult_v1.md` „ასაკის შესახებ ზოგადი კითხვა" section rewritten with the explicit 13-year floor + ban on the false „18 წლიდან" universal claim. 7 new tests.
- ✅ ~~Admin Panel saved event not visible to agent („ღონისძიება არ გვაქვს" while a valid event existed)~~ — Live QA Patch (2026-06-05). `_safe_load_yaml` documented as cache-free + adds mtime debug log so operator can verify the right file version is being read after Admin Panel save. `get_adult_events` no longer drops events with title-but-no-explicit-id (id auto-derived from index as `event_{idx}`). Combined with the 13-year floor fix, an operator-saved event with title + price now reaches the LLM tool layer on the next request — no server restart needed. 5 new tests.
- ✅ ~~CRITICAL: User selected „5 ივნისი 10:00" but agent confirmed „8 ივნისი 10:00"~~ — Live QA Patch (2026-06-05). `parent_flow._user_explicit_slot_choice` now uses a date-hint matcher: when the user message contains a date hint („5 ივნისი" / „ხვალ"), the matched slot MUST also match that date. With date hint but no offered match → returns None (deferring to `check_consultation_slot`). `parent_tool_executor._book_consultation` now compares the actual `lead.booked_datetime_iso` (read back after `_book_selected_slot`) against the requested ISO — mismatch yields `reason=slot_mismatch` with full state rollback (`calendly_booked` / `booked_datetime_iso` / `calendar_event_id` / `status`), Sentry capture with `area=booking` + slot ISO + actual ISO + masked sender, per-turn success flag forced False, `manager_handoff_required=True`. Success-path response derives `booked_date` / `booked_time` from the actual lead datetime, not the input args. `system_parent_v2.md` gains a „შერჩეული სლოტის წესი" section codifying the contract. 6 new tests + the pending-commit branch surfaces the brand-standard manager handoff line on `slot_mismatch`.

New `resolve_relative_datetime(text, *, now=None)` in `app/agent/services/timestamps.py` resolves Georgian relative-day phrases („ხვალ" / „ზეგ" / „დღეს" / „გუშინ" / „დღევანდელ" / „ხვალინდელ" / „გუშინწინ" / „მაზეგ") + optional Georgian time forms („11 საათზე" / „11-ზე" / „11:00") to a Tbilisi-aware datetime. `parent_llm_engine._build_context_message` now always surfaces `today_iso_tbilisi=YYYY-MM-DD` + `now_iso_tbilisi=...` + (when applicable) `resolved_relative_datetime_iso=...` so the LLM never has to guess today's date. `ParentToolExecutor` gets a `user_message` field + `_normalise_datetime_iso_from_message` helper that overrides the day of any stale LLM-supplied ISO when the user message has a relative-day phrase — wired into `check_consultation_slot`, `book_consultation`, and the reschedule branch of `manage_consultation_booking`. `system_parent_v2.md` gains a new top-level „თარიღების წესი" section codifying the rule + bans past-date wording for future relative dates. 30 new tests. **RESOLVED — Session 1 (2026-05-22 → 2026-05-31):**
- ✅ ~~Webhook signature unverified~~ — new `_verify_meta_signature(raw, header)` helper in [app/routes/webhook.py](ai-agent/app/routes/webhook.py) runs BEFORE the JSON parse + background task. HMAC-SHA256 keyed on `META_APP_SECRET` (alias `MESSENGER_APP_SECRET`) against the RAW request body; `hmac.compare_digest` for constant-time compare. Verification failure → 403 + `[webhook] signature rejected` log + NO background task. Fail-open shape: `VERIFY_WEBHOOK_SIGNATURE=True` AND empty secret → one warning + accept (protects existing 791-test suite that doesn't sign). Privacy: never logs raw body, header, computed digest, secret, or payload. 11 new tests in `tests/test_webhook_signature.py`.
- ✅ ~~Comment follow-up 400 retry-loop + Public reply not ready for App Review~~ — `sheets_service.get_pending_comment_followups` now requires `DM Sent==TRUE` AND `Status=="DMSent"` (was: `Status=="CommentOnly"` — the source of the hourly 400 spam). `comment_service.check_comment_followups` now skips senders that already have an active Conversation (DM follow-up owns the cadence), treats Meta HTTP 400 as permanent (one attempt, log once, mark row `Expired`, prime the existing `_processed_comments_lru` + Redis `processed_comment:<id>` guard so the same id is skipped on the next tick), and writes `FollowupSent` (was: unmapped `CommentFollowUp`) on success. HTTP 429/500/network exceptions keep the existing 3-attempt retry. `ENABLE_PUBLIC_COMMENT_REPLY` default flipped to True so the code is ready for Meta App Review approval — `.env` override to False still works. 27 new tests.

Three capture points: `conversation_service.process_message`, `parent_tool_executor.execute`, `followup_service.check_and_send_followups`. Privacy-safe context only (area, platform, tool, follow-up stage as string, masked sender id) — no raw user messages, no phone numbers, no full sender IDs, no tokens. `send_default_pii=False` hard-coded. Structured `[conversation] start/completed/error` + `[followup] sent/skipped/error reason=...` logs. 30 new tests.
